Company brief

MYE I MEDIA AS is a private limited company operating in Nyhetsbyråvirksomhet. Latest accounts (2025): revenue NOK 2.3m, annual result NOK 300.4k. Annual accounts available for 2011-2025 (15 years). Detailed records only available as PDFs. Unknowns: employees (registry returned no employee count); hiring (no job sources were checked).
